# Premium Telegram Emoji Catalog

Sections group emoji by topic and source pack. Read the relevant sections before selecting emoji.
Pack links are included so you can verify IDs or browse more options.
Reviewed pack names, categories, tags and notes: [pack analysis](pack-analysis.md) and `data/emoji-packs.json`.
